### 2026-10-26 — IMP-46 internal chat decision before implementation

- **User request:** an OS chat window; messages not viewed within five minutes trigger Telegram. Earlier instruction specifies one ping, at all times. Add this as IMP-46 while preserving the wider roadmap.
- **Business/workflow:** start with persistent direct conversations between active teammates. Each message has its own recipient/read time and a retry-safe send identifier. This removes the need to attach ordinary team communication to a delivery task. Group membership and attachments are separate later extensions, not implied working features.
- **Authority/data:** normalized chat history, participant-only reads (including administrators), active workspace/session checks, no message content in audit/Telegram. The teammate picker exposes names only. Sending/viewing/retry and access revocation are checked server-side; no client timestamp can suppress a reminder for another message.
- **UI/UX:** an explicitly named Team chat area distinct from client inbox and AI chat, conversation list, readable bubbles, unread/viewed indicators, bounded older history and protected drafts. Mark only inbound messages actually visible in a focused window; opening a conversation must not clear all unread messages.
- **Reminders:** reuse the durable Telegram outbox/worker and its uncertain-delivery handling, with an independent chat due-message scan. Five minutes is the threshold, delivered on the next scheduler pass. Linked recipient/bot/worker setup is required; show missing setup honestly. No recurring pings for the same message and no quiet-hour restriction.
- **Acceptance:** migration up/down, service authorization/idempotency/concurrent read-vs-claim checks, fake-clock reminders with no real Telegram traffic, UI visibility/draft/pagination tests and desktop/mobile browser checks. Existing message/reminder behavior must remain passing.

### 2026-10-26 — IMP-46 internal team chat implementation and acceptance

### 2026-10-26 — IMP-39 conversation role access decision

- **Business outcome/workflow:** the founder can currently share a client conversation with individual people only. Add explicitly selected team-role grants per conversation so staffing changes follow assigned roles, with separate viewing and reply preparation. Preserve direct grants and owner access. Show the combined access for every active person, including the grant source; removing one grant must not imply access is removed if another role, direct grant or administrator permission still allows it.
- **Alternatives and scope:** a workspace-wide inbox capability would expose unrelated clients, and copying project membership would violate the founder's selected-conversation requirement. Extend each conversation's existing explicit allowlist with optional role grants, resolved against current assignments in the same transaction as each read/write. Empty or absent role grants retain existing behavior. This is a scoped access improvement, not email/Slack/WhatsApp synchronization or authorization to send externally.
- **Privacy/design:** private means owner-only, including against administrator/role grants. Shared conversations retain existing administrator access. The form separates individual and role grants, explains that roles include future assigned members, and previews effective permissions rather than implying unchecked boxes are explicit denials. Hide stale role-directory data on errors, preserve drafts, and require a valid fresh directory before saving role access. The conversation detail uses server-calculated draft permission so UI and AI draft eligibility cannot disagree.
- **Cross-system acceptance:** validate role IDs inside the workspace, duplicate/inconsistent grants, fresh multiple-role unions, revocation, private override, unauthorized readers/writers, AI completion after access removal and activity evidence. Keep permission changes transactional and exclude message contents from activity. Verify frontend errors/draft preservation/preview and desktop/narrow layouts with synthetic isolated data, not real membership changes. IMP-39 remains Partial for project/client and agent scopes beyond this slice.

### 2026-10-26 — IMP-39 conversation role access implementation and acceptance

- **Implemented:** each client conversation can grant viewing and reply preparation to explicitly selected team roles, in addition to individual people. Current assigned role IDs are resolved server-side even when the role has no global capabilities. Multiple role/direct grants combine; removing one grant does not negate another. Private conversations remain owner-only, including against administrators. Existing records keep their behavior, and older clients that omit the new field cannot silently erase saved role grants.
- **Workflow and permissions:** the server validates unique workspace role IDs and disallows reply grants without viewing. Current authority is checked on snapshots, note/draft actions and AI preparation/completion. Conversation-specific draft permission comes from the server rather than a separate frontend interpretation. Revocation removes the conversation, notes, draft permissions and residual seen-state IDs from the next response. No project, phone or financial authority is granted. Role changes are recorded with role-name snapshots in the existing transactional activity ledger; message text stays excluded.

### 2026-10-26 — IMP-18 meeting knowledge decision before implementation

- **User requirement/business outcome:** after a brand meeting, staff can save a Fathom/recording link and paste a transcript or notes. Reviewed decisions become current brand context for subsequent assistant work; proposed task/plan changes must cite the meeting and remain reviewable. This reduces lost client decisions and repeated manual briefing without treating an imperfect transcript as an automatic command.
- **Workflow/design:** add a Brand knowledge destination beside client work. Select the client project, add a titled/date-stamped source and the concise knowledge to publish, then a manager publishes the reviewed revision. Distinguish raw meeting evidence, draft interpretation and approved context. Show provenance, version, approval and stale/withdrawn state; preserve drafts and reject stale edits. Retain the last approved version while a replacement is drafted. Existing final-delivery approval and commitments remain unchanged.
- **Scope/security:** begin with explicit project-scoped knowledge associated with the brand; do not silently share confidential meeting content across all company projects. Raw transcripts are manager/author-only; published context is available to that project's authorized staff and assistants. Use normalized, versioned records, read-only bounded lists and transactional structural audit events without transcript bodies. Untrusted source text cannot grant tools, change permissions or authorize external actions.
- **Agent use:** include bounded approved meeting context and source/version references in task assistant previews/runs. Detect changed/withdrawn knowledge before dispatch and result acceptance, so old recommendations are not silently applied. Existing suggestion review applies; unrestricted task/plan mutation remains IMP-23 and is not claimed here.
- **External integration:** Fathom's official Public API guidance (https://help.fathom.video/en/articles/8368641, checked 2026-10-26) supports transcripts/summaries and says user API keys see owned/team-shared meetings, not every unshared meeting. Paste/manual capture needs no connection. Link-only records must say transcript not imported. Automatic Fathom import needs an authorized account and verified API contract; no web scraping or arbitrary URL fetching. Generic video transcription needs a selected, budgeted provider; no extra spend authorized.
- **Acceptance:** source/approval/version flow, contributor versus manager access, cross-project/tenant denial, stale edits, safe transcript rendering, draft recovery, bounded agent context and revoked/updated knowledge checks. Inspect desktop/narrow screens and run rollback/provider-mock checks before recording completion. IMP-18 remains partial until the broader facts/claims/references/assets library is complete.

### 2026-10-26 — IMP-02 navigation decision before implementation

- **Business outcome:** editors/designers need production shortcuts, marketers need advertising, SEO staff need recurring work, Amna needs planning/review and the founder needs company context. Opening a generic Today page and expanding several groups adds repeated navigation work.
- **Workflow:** verified role assignments → suggested start page and shortcuts → optional personal start page/pins → direct navigation. Explicit URLs (including task/filter links) always win. Role changes filter now-unavailable preferences immediately; an inaccessible explicit URL retains the existing unavailable-state behavior.
- **Alternatives:** hiding most modules would obscure legitimate multi-role work and confuse preferences with security. A new dashboard per job adds duplicate maintenance. Keep the existing grouped sidebar and mobile selector; add a small Pinned section and a focused Personalize workspace panel with a start-page selector and bounded allowed-page checklist. Keep all permitted areas discoverable.
- **Role defaults:** derive suggestions from stable built-in assigned role IDs and effective capabilities, never from editable role labels. Combine multi-role shortcuts deterministically. Custom roles fall back to capability-based defaults; personal choices override suggestions without altering grants. Existing IMP-39 capability checks are sufficient for navigation suggestions; its broader pending server scopes remain separately tracked.
- **Storage/tradeoff:** use existing Jotai/browser-preference patterns, partitioned by signed-in user/workspace/membership, storing only view IDs. Clearly label preferences as browser-local. Validate persisted input and intersect with the permitted destination catalog. No migration or new authorization system is warranted for this slice; cross-device preferences remain a future enhancement, not an implied capability.
- **Design/acceptance:** visible start-page/current-page naming, accessible selection/pin controls, compact desktop shortcuts and mobile pinned options. Verify founder/secondary-admin/manager/editor/marketing/SEO/designer and multi-role defaults, custom-role fallback, reset, reload persistence, identity separation, revoked access, deep links/back history, keyboard and 390px layout. Revisit existing IMP-01/10 navigation behavior so preferences cannot discard unsaved work or unexpectedly redirect a task link. No provider calls or changed business permissions.

Remove pinned pages from the remaining grouped menu and mobile options. Group headings now expand their own destinations instead of unexpectedly navigating to the first page; single remaining destinations stay direct links. Preserve automatic expansion of the current destination, keyboard-native disclosure behavior and all permitted pages. This resolves a visible hierarchy problem before acceptance, without changing business access.

- **IMP-02/10 browser-found timing gap:** a rapid mobile project-name edit followed immediately by the workspace selector bypassed the draft prompt, while the same steps with an intervening observation were protected. ### 2026-10-27 — IMP-10/39 project conflict protection decision

- **Company/workflow:** Amna and the founder can edit a project's client and members at the same time. Add a monotonic integer revision to the existing normalized project table, defaulting existing projects to version 1, and compare/increment under the existing transaction/access lock. This is a bounded correctness fix, not the independent-write refactor in IMP-34.
- **Design:** preserve familiar project/member controls and the existing conflict banner/Keep-or-Discard dialog. A save failure refreshes the project snapshot when possible without replacing typed fields. Clean and dirty stale forms both require an explicit reload before another save. Record the revision transition in activity so changes can be traced.

No real staff access is changed for testing.

### 2026-10-27 — IMP-10/39 project conflict acceptance

### 2026-10-27 — IMP-16 capacity planning decision

- **Outcome/workflow:** Amna needs to see editor and reviewer overload before promising another batch. A manager records each task's remaining production minutes, revision allowance, expected generation wait, review minutes, complexity and planned review day. Existing scheduled task dates own the production date. The planner compares that work to explicitly entered daily availability; people with both production and review assignments use one shared daily allowance.
- **Alternatives:** counting videos or assigning an automatic per-video duration would hide the difference between originals, variants and complex scenes. Automatic rescheduling would make commitments without manager judgment. Use estimates in minutes and explicit calendar dates, with no invented working week, salaries, utilization score or default availability. Blank means unknown; zero availability means unavailable. Estimates are planning assumptions, never inferred hours worked.
- **Design:** one compact Team planner capacity section, separate from task filters so a filtered list cannot imply spare team capacity. Seven-day date range in the URL, per-person daily rows, clear overload/incomplete estimates, direct links back to affected tasks. Task detail owns one guarded planning editor; reviewers and editors can see assumptions, while managers edit them. Production/revision and review consume capacity; generation wait is displayed separately and is not deducted as active labor. Missing/past dates stay in a visible needs-planning queue rather than silently moving to today. Approved work is excluded; work in review consumes only the remaining review estimate.
- **Architecture/tradeoffs:** normalized member/date availability rows with optimistic versions and atomic activity. Task planning remains on the existing versioned task, with a dedicated planning command that cannot change brief, assignments or approval. Typed availability API, fresh active-manager authorization, workspace isolation and stale-draft protection. The current complete task snapshot is reused for aggregation; its scaling replacement stays IMP-37. Multi-day effort must be split into dated tasks or explicitly rescheduled; no pretend optimization solver.
- **Acceptance:** unknown versus zero, combined production/review roles, revision allowance, waits, review/approved stages, missing dates, overload, immutable aggregation, stale/concurrent edits, revoked/foreign identities, transaction rollback and read-only snapshots. Inspect real desktop/mobile planning, input validation, failed save/reload, draft navigation and task links before completion. Existing batch lifecycle and independent review rules remain unchanged; IMP-15's verified task-based approach is suitable and needs no reopening.

### 2026-10-27 — IMP-16 final local acceptance

- Managers can enter exact daily working availability and remaining task effort, revision allowance, expected generation wait, review effort/day and complexity. The seven-day Team planner combines production and review for the same person, shows overload and incomplete assumptions, keeps missing/past dates visible, and links directly to tasks. Task-list filters do not hide capacity commitments. No quota equates originals with variations. Approved work is excluded; review-stage work counts only its remaining review effort. Unset availability/estimates remain unknown; zero is an explicit choice.
- Normalized member/date rows have optimistic versions and per-slot locks under the shared access lock. Independent availability changes can proceed together; revoked manager access cannot race a save. ### 2026-10-27 — IMP-19 revision economics decision

- **Outcome/workflow:** avoid treating every returned video as the same kind of failure. Record a durable round when review requests changes, client fulfilment is marked Redoing, or approved/reviewed creative/workflow requirements are reopened. Keep the initiating reason and source, then let managers classify internal defect, client preference, scope change or other and record actual revision effort. Finance-authorized people can record internal cost and an optional proposed client charge, default zero.
- **Alternatives/tradeoffs:** one mutable creative `revisionCharge` loses past rounds and has no currency. Reusing cash receipts would falsely imply money was collected or billed. Use normalized per-task rounds and explicit currency/minor-unit amounts; preserve the old field as legacy information rather than guessing its currency. A planned charge is never an invoice, retainer change, payment or profitability calculation. Cost summaries separate causes/currencies and expose missing costs; full salary/overhead allocation remains IMP-21.
- **Design:** show readable rounds, source, reason, effort and saved corrections inside the existing task, with financial fields hidden unless the current finance capability permits them. Use guarded forms and expected versions; keep an immutable activity trail. A finance summary links back to affected work and labels recorded revision cost separately from cash. Current submission history stays intact. Earlier unclassified work stays visibly incomplete rather than fabricated as classified historical rounds.
- **Acceptance:** every reopen path creates one round atomically with its task transition; repeated/stale requests cannot duplicate it. Check task/project/workspace access, finance read/edit separation and revocation, zero-charge defaults, unknown costs/effort, amount/currency validation, stale corrections and rollback. Verify cost summaries never combine currencies or silently treat missing costs as zero; inspect task/history/report journeys at desktop/mobile. This is active engineering, not completion or a request for approval.

### 2026-10-27 — IMP-21 monthly management-finance decision

- **Outcome/current workflow:** current Finance tracks receipt-date cash and one-project cash contribution. Monthly employee pay, overhead and service-period revenue need explicit recognition and allocation before the founder can assess client contribution. Cash receipts must not become earned revenue automatically, and paying an obligation must not recognize its cost twice.
- **Chosen first complete journey:** create editable monthly salary/overhead plans → generate a reviewable month draft once per plan/month → review the exact amount/effective month/source version → allocate costs or revenue to client projects or an explicit company/unassigned bucket → post the monthly entry → inspect currency-separated company and client recorded results. Manual revenue/direct costs require explicit month/evidence; existing cash/receivable behavior stays available in its own Finance view. Missing sources/allocations remain visible and do not silently count as zero or fully loaded profit.
- **Architecture/tradeoffs:** use normalized cost-plan, monthly-recognition and allocation tables with exact integer micro-unit strings, immutable posted source snapshots, optimistic revisions, atomic activity and per-period/source concurrency guards. Do not infer hours from clicks, capacity or revision estimates, guess an equal split, auto-prorate salaries, generate revenue from prose retainers, convert currencies or assume tax treatment. Plan changes affect new drafts, never posted history. Existing salary cash payments and revision diagnostics are not added automatically to recognized costs.
- **Design:** retain one Finance destination, with explicit Cash records and Monthly results/cost planning views; preserve URLs, guarded drafts, read/edit finance capabilities and source details. Show reviewable drafts separately from posted actual entries, clear allocation totals and an unassigned-company row. Display exact source identity and correction/void history; server failures retain edits. Inspect founder/Amna and finance-reader journeys on desktop/mobile before recording acceptance.
- **Limits/next scope:** this begins IMP-21, not whole-item completion. AI settled-cost import and late adjustments, source-linked cash recognition/reconciliation, payment/collection forecasts, closing controls and configurable accountant/Zoho export mappings still require subsequent engineering. Real employee rates, balances and mapping choices belong in editable setup; they do not block this local build.

Finance design refinement: expected due days support 1–31 with an explicit shorter-month last-day rule, so month-end obligations do not require a false day-28 date. Drafts may retain a visible unallocated remainder; posting requires exact allocation conservation. A departed employee may still be owed historical pay: immutable drafts expose current-source warnings and require a fresh source-review token plus explicit acknowledgement, instead of silently erasing the obligation or blocking it forever. These changes do not initiate payroll or payments.

### 2026-10-27 — IMP-21 payment reconciliation decision

- **Outcome/current gap:** current cash confirmation copies the full receivable into a receipt dated today; it cannot express partial payments and does not connect receipt timing to explicitly recognized monthly results. Founder/Amna need to see actual payment, invoice balance and service-month result without counting the same money twice.
- **Chosen workflow:** record an amount/date/evidence payment or select an existing immutable receipt → apply explicit same-currency amounts to receivables → separately match cash to posted monthly allocations → inspect paid/unpaid/unmatched remainders and the full source chain → reverse a mistaken relation with a reason. Matching never creates another cash movement or recognizes revenue/cost. No FX, fees, refund policy or automatic revenue-recognition rule is guessed.
- **Alternatives/tradeoffs:** blindly turning receipts into revenue would double count service periods; replacing all Operations domains would delay the usable workflow. Add normalized source references, receivable settlements and monthly cash matches alongside the existing cash adapter. Preserve legacy full-payment receipts and statuses, and account for them once; partial payments must also update existing cash receivable summaries accurately. Source snapshots/tokens, idempotency and exact integer amounts protect stale review and audit history.
- **Design:** keep one Finance destination with a plain-language Payments and matching view. Select a payment, see its amount/date/direction and available balance, then allocate deliberately with source links and clear warnings. Invoice settlement and monthly recognition matches have separate conserved remainders. Show wrong-currency/direction/client states, unavailable/stale source, read-only permissions, empty, success, error and draft recovery. Inspect desktop/mobile partial payment → balance → monthly match → reversal/void before acceptance.
- **System effects/acceptance:** exclusive access lock must precede source/period locks when crossing legacy cash and normalized monthly finance. Cash void reverses its links without voiding recognized results; monthly void reverses its matches without altering actual payments. Protect partially settled invoices from ordinary voiding. Verify exact conservation, compatibility, idempotency, concurrency, void races, permissions/revocation, read-only snapshots and unchanged adjacent cash/monthly totals. Real usage requires actual payment evidence and staff-selected periods; automatic recognition/accountant mappings remain future explicit setup.

### 2026-10-28 — IMP-10/21 full-application outage recovery finding

- **Observed material gap:** the actual API-stop test replaced the whole workspace with ClientConfigProvider's global backend-unreachable fallback. That unmounted in-memory forms and required a page reload, defeating otherwise verified domain-level draft recovery. The shared recovery portion of IMP-10 remains Partial; the payment-reconciliation slice is not accepted until this path is verified.

Keep the healthy layout unchanged and restore a logical focus target. Cached private figures must never remain visibly accessible during the outage. Prefer this bounded provider correction over persisting sensitive finance drafts to local storage or adding a new offline write queue.
- **Acceptance:** prove initial failure gating, healthy → outage → retry with same draft, hidden/inaccessible stale content, repeated failure/retry, current identity/revocation boundaries and unchanged layout. Repeat desktop/mobile payment matching after recovery and verify no unrequested write occurs. Heavy validation remains sequential; the user authorized a 6–8 GB VM allocation and the resumed VM uses 6 GB.

### 2026-10-28 — IMP-21 recorded AI-cost import decision

- **Business outcome/current gap:** production-AI charges have an exact budget ledger but are not included in monthly client/company results. Manually copying an administrator's budget total risks treating reservations or the conservative highest reported exposure as costs, duplicating a charge and losing its source. Founder/Amna need reviewable source-linked costs and explicit allocation without changing the paid-AI ledger or actual cash.
- **Current → proposed workflow:** an operator with finance management opens Finance → AI costs → chooses an immutable settled/reconciled or later usage evidence event → inspects its basis and exact USD amount → chooses service month and client/company allocations → saves a source-linked draft → reviews and posts it separately. Unknown, active, released or malformed sources explain why they cannot be imported. Existing finance readers can inspect the safe financial source projection. Import never dispatches a model or moves money.
- **Alternatives and chosen approach:** do not sum budget exposure, automatically recognize every run, or import the same charge again for another month. Extend monthly entries with a distinct immutable AI source and one normalized workspace/charge claim. One initial draft/posted entry claims the charge. A pending correction draft may coexist with the prior posted entry; only explicit correction posting atomically voids the old recognized cost and posts the replacement. This supports lower as well as higher costs without negative costs or invented revenue. A separate negative-adjustment ledger would add reporting semantics and sign handling unsupported by current finance; silent editing of posted entries would destroy auditability.
- **Evidence and authority:** original settlement and later reported usage remain separately selectable financial evidence, not proof of a provider invoice or a final corrected bill. Recorded usage costs use the saved administrator-verified tariff; manual reconciliation is identified as administrator evidence. Preserve the selected event, original amount/model/source version and replacement reason. Exact zero and sub-cent USD values are valid. Reservations, unknown amounts and cap high-water marks are never imported. No exchange rate is invented, and unsupported/native CRM charges are not represented as covered.
- **UI/UX and design:** add a focused AI costs view inside the existing Finance navigation and a contextual link from Monthly results. Use a bounded source list and one inline review/allocation panel with a visible next action; avoid a nested modal containing source pagination, exceptions and a long form. Stack controls at 390px, retain source/filter/month URLs and draft protection, and distinguish initial import, already imported, changed evidence, pending correction and unavailable sources. Posting a correction must name the prior month/entry and explicitly acknowledge its void and any reversed monthly cash matches; actual cash/invoice balances stay unchanged. Existing entry history and source links remain the way to inspect posted results.
- **System effects/tradeoffs:** keep the production budget ledger immutable and its spending-cap behavior unchanged. Finance read/manage capabilities govern safe financial projections and recognition; importing a cost does not require granting budget administration. Independent review rejected inheriting the budget screen's administrator gate because it would unnecessarily prevent an authorized finance operator from doing bookkeeping. Budget setup and uncertain-charge reconciliation remain administrator-only; finance projections exclude private task names, prompts, raw usage/pricing/provider payloads, arbitrary attention reasons and private reconciliation evidence. Recheck current identity/finance authority before ordered delivery/source/period/entry locks; avoid lock upgrades. Unique claims and stable request IDs prevent duplicate active recognition across months, including retries after edits/voids. Posting requires a fresh source/claim/correction review; concurrent later evidence, claim changes, cash matches and revoked access must fail safely. Claim/source relations need generated additive schema changes with rollback. This bounded import does not complete forecasts, monthly closing, accountant exports or provider invoice reconciliation.
- **Acceptance before completion:** test settled and manually reconciled sources, multiple higher/lower reports, missing/malformed/foreign evidence, exact zero/sub-cent/large values, explicit allocations and duplicates; prove retry uniqueness, stale previews, pending correction cancellation, atomic replacement/reversal, revocation, private-field redaction and unchanged budget/cash/invoice records. Run actual-service rollback and independent-session races with disposable fixtures, direct types/lint and migration checks. Inspect source → draft → post → correction/history plus errors/outage/revocation and desktop/390px behavior. Root schedules bounded heavy validation; delegated implementation follows this review. IMP-21 remains In progress and whole-item counts are unchanged.

### 2026-10-28 — IMP-21 monthly result-locking decision

- **Business outcome and workflow:** founder/Amna need stable reviewed monthly results while bookkeeping and late provider evidence continue. Finance operator reviews a month across separate currency totals → resolves its drafts/pending replacement drafts → previews the recorded result and limitations → locks it with a reason. Later recognition changes require a deliberate reasoned reopen, then edits and a new review/lock. The observable success check is that a locked month's recognized entries/totals cannot change through any ordinary or AI import path, while actual payment and reconciliation work remains available.
- **Alternatives/chosen approach:** a visual badge alone provides no guarantee; freezing cash, invoice settlement or incoming AI evidence would obstruct real operations. Add one normalized workspace/month result-period state and immutable lock/reopen events, using existing finance permissions and transaction infrastructure. A missing period is Open without creating data during reads. Locking is a management review control, not a tax/accounting close or a claim that all costs were entered. More restrictive reopening or independent closing approval would introduce business policy not supplied by the user.
- **UI/UX/design:** extend Monthly results with a compact Open/Locked summary, reviewer/time and a clearly named review action. Show currency-separated totals, unresolved drafts and cross-month corrections that affect the month before locking; require them to be posted or voided. Explain unprepared plans/unrecognized AI as limitations because their service month cannot be inferred. Use a focused inline review/reason panel, explicit lock/reopen action and bounded history; preserve month/filter URLs, keyboard access, narrow layout, stale/access/outage recovery and draft protection. Do not add another top-level sidebar destination.
- **System effects/tradeoffs:** enforce current finance.read/manage under delivery access and sorted exclusive period locks in prepare-month, manual save/post/void and AI prepare/post (both original and replacement periods). Do not gate shared reversal helpers: cash voiding, cash matches/reversals, invoices, actual payments, plan configuration and incoming AI evidence must remain usable without changing recognized results. Lock/reopen recomputes a fresh preview token and stores exact result-summary/hash, attribution and reason; stable command IDs and optimistic revisions protect retries/concurrency. Match totals are live reconciliation information outside the frozen recognition summary. Schema changes require a generated reversible instance command and rollback guard for lock history.
- **Acceptance required:** actual-service checks of every recognition mutation, zero/multiple currencies, drafts/cross-month corrections, stale previews, idempotence, authority revocation, payment/match/ledger preservation, lock-versus-post/correction races and generated migration rollback. Inspect desktop/390px review → blocked draft → lock → blocked edit → reopen → correction → relock/history plus outage/permission recovery. Delegate source work with bounded root-run validation; implementation is active, not accepted or counted complete.
- **Concurrency design refinement:** independent review found that making ordinary prepare/save/post take exclusive delivery access would regress the existing independent-month concurrency guarantee. Preserve their shared delivery access with an exclusive target-period lock and current Open check. Cash-reversing void, AI cross-ledger changes and lock/reopen transitions retain exclusive delivery access; transitions therefore wait for active ordinary writes before recomputing their preview. This keeps the lock boundary authoritative without serializing unrelated monthly bookkeeping. Generated schema drift was trimmed to the one period table/index/workspace FK; downgrade refuses both period records and retained PERIOD history.

### 2026-10-28 — IMP-21 reviewed cash-forecast decision

- **Business outcome/current → proposed workflow:** founder/Amna need to see whether recorded cash and expected collections cover planned salary/overhead payments, with accountable follow-up. Today the cash summary is historical and recognition is a separate result. Add Finance → Cash forecast: confirm dated starting cash for one currency → schedule outstanding invoice collections with an eligible owner/next action → confirm dated plan-month or one-off outflows → inspect a 30/90-day daily closing projection and unresolved items → link actual expense/salary payments to expectations and retain their history. A useful acceptance scenario is a USD 1,000 invoice with a USD 400 receipt: cash rises once, expected collection becomes USD 600, and monthly matching changes neither.
- **Alternatives/chosen scope:** collections-only would help debt chasing but would not answer salary/overhead coverage, so implement the bounded end-to-end cash plan. Reusing historical Number-based cash summaries, automatically treating every recognized COST/AI charge as payable, or summing active budgets would misstate cash. Keep exact integer micros/currencies and explicitly confirmed expectations. This is a reviewed scenario of recorded information, not a complete balance sheet, bank synchronization, guaranteed runway or automatic financial advice. No probabilities, automatic client messages, money movement or invented payment terms.
- **Starting cash/calendar:** normalize one reviewed cash anchor per workspace/currency, including explicit zero/negative amounts, closing-through date, reporting IANA timezone, evidence/note, revision and attributed history. Missing cash is unknown, not zero. Because existing cash dates have day precision, require a completed closing date before the current business date in the chosen timezone. Derive today's business date from that confirmed zone; do not offer arbitrary historical as-of balances using today's settlement totals. Add only subsequent recorded actual INCOME/EXPENSE/SALARY movements through today, once; old OPENING_BALANCE records and movements covered by the anchor are excluded. Later backdated records through the anchor date are assumed covered by its confirmed closing balance and are disclosed as such.
- **Collections and ownership:** reuse manager-scoped validated cash/invoice readers, partial settlements and legacy receipt compatibility. One source-linked schedule per invoice initially expects the full remaining balance on one explicit date; contractual due date remains separate. Overdue/undated collections require a reviewed expected date, never silently move to today. Keep an active eligible finance reader as owner, a next-action date and note; show revoked/departed owners as needing reassignment, without granting access. The actionable finance worklist is the first follow-up surface; no external send or pretend native task creation occurs. Generic task/escalation integration belongs to the subsequent founder-risk workflow.
- **Outgoing expectations:** create one confirmed plan/month occurrence using existing month-end due-day clamping as a suggestion, or a reviewed manual outflow. Plan/recognition changes cannot generate duplicate expectations or establish actual payment. Existing expense/salary payments may be partially applied through a separate conserved realization relation; ordinary monthly recognition matches remain independent. Actual cash is counted once regardless of matching. Applying/reversing a realization adjusts only remaining expected outflow; voiding actual cash reverses its realization links atomically, even when monthly results are locked. Cancellation/revision needs a reason; amounts cannot be reduced below applied payments. Zero-cost assumptions are explicit, not unknown.
- **Temporal/source limits:** current receivable totals include all active settlements, so future-dated, invalid or legacy-paid-without-receipt sources cannot silently produce reliable dated collections. Reuse structural source snapshots/tokens, and surface source changes, future cash, missing sources, unassigned/ineligible owners, undated/overdue expectations and unapplied actual outflows as an actionable exceptions queue. Quarantine unreliable expectations from the dated scenario, show that it is incomplete and distinguish unknown amounts from known unscheduled amounts. Routine legitimate payments update the live remaining balance; structural source changes require another review. Do not convert historical dates between timezones or invent exchange rates.
- **Data/authority/reliability:** normalized anchor, source-linked forecast item and outflow-realization entities with workspace-safe relations/uniqueness, exact amounts, optimistic revisions, stable command identities and immutable finance events. Extract reusable manager-scoped readers instead of calling separately transactional services or combining paginated browser responses. Recheck current finance read/manage and eligible owners; serialize cross-ledger changes with existing delivery/source/item locks in a consistent order. Forecast writes do not change recognized results, invoices, actual cash or AI budgets and remain usable while recognition is locked. Generate reversible additive migrations with history-preserving rollback guards.
- **UI/UX/design:** stay inside Finance rather than adding sidebar clutter. Prioritize selected currency, business date/timezone, 30/90-day horizon and freshness; then confirmed recorded cash → expected collections/outflows → reviewed ending balance/first negative daily close. Place incomplete-data warnings and the owner/next-action worklist before the dated timeline. Keep currencies separate and daily net closing balances explicit; no intraday liquidity ordering is claimed. Use compact summary/list patterns and focused inline forms with source links, clear initial setup, empty/loading/error/success, stale/revoked-access recovery, URL filters, unsaved-work protection, keyboard access and 390px layouts. Editable real balances/rates/owners belong in setup and do not block local engineering.
- **Acceptance before completion:** partial/legacy collections, dated current cash versus future cash, anchor coverage/backdating, zero/negative/sub-cent/large values, separated currencies, month-end plan suggestions, unique source occurrences, partial outflow realizations/reversals/cash voids, source amendments/cancellation, role/owner revocation, idempotence, atomic audit failure and independent-session races. Prove unchanged cash/recognition/budget records on forecast-only writes and no double counting between settlement, monthly matching and realization. Inspect desktop/390px setup → schedule → projection → follow-up/review → actual payment realization/history plus stale/outage recovery and cleanup. IMP-21 remains In progress; this decision authorizes the next implementation slice without changing whole-item counts.
- **Source-boundary refinement:** existing financial entries are immutable in BusinessService and changed normalized invoice sources are rejected by payment reconciliation. Preserve that boundary: invoice amount/currency corrections use void/new source, then cancel the old forecast and schedule the corrected invoice. A forecast-only override would falsely suggest a repair while payment settlement remained blocked. Mutable cost plans differ: an existing occurrence can be explicitly rereviewed with current source terms, reason and warning acknowledgement after plan deactivation/range changes; initial occurrence creation remains eligible-source-only and no payable is silently removed or reset.
- **Cash/debt separation:** valid dated actual cash remains part of recorded cash even when its invoice allocation is inconsistent. Quarantine the affected invoice's remaining amount as unknown and surface the allocation anomaly; never erase the real receipt, silently use zero debt or infer an unrecorded receipt from a PAID label. The manager-scoped reader reuses the defensive cash adapter and legacy receipt target, but does not reuse the historical helper's unsupported paid-without-receipt assumption. Structural review tokens ignore routine receipt balances/revision/PAID/project-name changes and still detect void/financial-source changes.
- **Initial calculation evidence:** the pure exact projection passed 14 cases including 400 received/600 remaining, anchor-covered and future cash exclusion, signed/zero/sub-cent/large aggregates, horizon boundaries, first negative daily close/recovery, same-day net and leap/DST/year boundaries. The source projection passed 23 cases including missing/future/void/invalid receipts, normalized/legacy proof, over/double allocation without cash loss, currency isolation, private-payload exclusion, immutable-source mismatches, duplicate IDs and stable structural tokens. Whole forecast acceptance remains open until schema, service/concurrency and actual UI checks pass.

### 2026-10-28 — IMP-21 accountant-export decision

- **Business outcome/current → proposed workflow:** Amna/founder need a reproducible, reviewable handoff to their accountant without re-entering income and costs or confusing delivery forecasts with accounting transactions. The existing browser CSV is a raw record dump, including receivables/opening/void records, and is not a bookkeeping import. Add Finance → Accounting exports: configure a reusable profile → select a currency and source period → review mappings, exclusions, exact totals and source changes → save an immutable batch → download identical bytes on retry → explicitly record a later manual import with evidence. Keep any raw-record download clearly labelled.
- **Alternatives and chosen scope:** implement a generic accountant handoff with editable template headers/field mappings and account/payment-account/counterparty mappings. Keep actual cash and posted service-month recognition as separate profile bases; do not turn invoices, plans, forecasts, settlements or monthly matches into extra transactions. ### 2026-10-28 — IMP-22 founder exception workflow decision

- **Business outcome/workflow:** the founder and Amna need to find the client/work/cash/campaign problem, inspect dated evidence and reach the responsible person's actual next action. Replace the four browser-computed counts in Company overview with a scoped, typed, source-linked attention queue. Reuse authoritative tasks, verified commitments, reviewed finance and supported campaign reviews; do not create a second task/finance ledger or infer client sentiment, hours or a health score. An empty filtered queue means no known matching exceptions, with unavailable/incomplete domains shown explicitly.
- **Alternatives/chosen approach:** another dashboard of counts would preserve the investigation burden; duplicating data into triage records would drift from approvals/fulfilment and cash. Use a read-only typed query with bounded source reads, source/version identities, one factual reason per stable key, explicit coverage and typed action targets. Combine related reasons per source where practical in the UI. Pending review is review work, not automatically an overdue failure. Missing owners and unknown balances remain unknown; never choose an arbitrary project member or guess a risk/low-margin threshold. No new entity is needed for this queue.
- **Scope/authority:** preserve the existing Company overview administrator boundary, including current native membership and dynamic roles. Finance evidence additionally requires current finance.read; restrictions must not leak counts, amounts or client names. Derive overdue/blocked/revision/review evidence from actual state, ended commitment deficits from current-version fulfilment accounting, financial exceptions from exact reviewed forecasts/collections and recorded monthly results, and campaign review/readiness only from currently supported reports. Unavailable unified goals/outcomes under IMP-28 remain a visible coverage limit. Reads and refresh do not mutate source records or send notifications. Recheck identity/access around source collection and use stable bounded cursors; detect overflow instead of silently calling a truncated result complete.
- **UI/UX/design:** keep the existing Company overview destination with a compact Needs attention heading, observed time and Refresh. Show labelled URL filters, bounded results, explicit missing coverage and stacked rows: risk/client → dated evidence/exact currency → accountable owner or Unassigned → one plain next-action link. Secondary detail holds source version/provenance. Keep connection readiness below the actionable work. Use mobile-stacked controls and keyboard-accessible links; verify empty/loading/error/revocation/retry states. Existing task/finance/report links are reused. Client engagement/commitment period selection currently lives in React state, so add guarded URL selection for exact source links rather than pretending a generic Clients link opens the right promise.
- **Escalation tradeoff/remaining scope:** founder Telegram escalation needs explicit enabled categories, recipient, urgency/scope and repeat policy using the existing durable outbox. Do not turn every review or missing-data row into an automatic alert or invent financial thresholds. The queue is a first slice; IMP-22 stays In progress until its escalation/ownership/acceptance requirements are met. Existing live Telegram connection/recipient setup remains a real external prerequisite.
- **Acceptance:** factual reasons, deduplication, current-version commitments and exact finance/currency/unknown behavior; admin/secondary-admin/current revocation and finance denial; complete-filter counts/bounded cursor/overflow; exact source/deep links and retained navigation; no writes or sends on reads; meaningful service/UI checks and desktop/390px journeys. Root owns one memory-bounded heavy-check queue; backend, frontend and source-link work proceed independently.

### 2026-10-28 — IMP-22 configurable escalation decision

- **Business outcome/workflow:** founder/Amna choose which factual risks warrant Telegram interruption, who receives them and when an unresolved issue may repeat. Build rules inside Company overview: preview current matching evidence → save paused → explicitly enable and adopt rule routing → observe issue/notification receipts → acknowledge to stop repeats without falsely resolving the source. Keep the actionable queue and employee five-minute/chat/fulfilment reminders intact.
- **Chosen bounded design:** three normalized concepts—versioned rule, observed source-issue episode and immutable notification occurrence—reuse the existing durable outbox, messaging worker and Telegram sender. Do not place financial/campaign pseudo-task notices or growing rule/history payloads in Operations JSON. A tiny versioned routing marker in the existing Operations configuration records explicit legacy-to-rules adoption, persisting even when all rules are paused. No enabled rule, founder recipient or bot connection is invented.
- **Rule semantics:** one explicit supported condition per rule, active administrator recipient, chosen NORMAL/URGENT priority, all-versus-selected client scope with explicit company/unassigned inclusion, optional currency scope and an explicit service month for monthly conditions. Immediate evaluation runs24/7; choose once per observed issue or a bounded interval/max-count repeat policy. Only successful prior sends can qualify for scheduled repeats; failed/uncertain sends cannot become automatic retries. Acknowledgement stops repeats but does not change tasks, fulfilment or finance. Resolution notifications and automatic financial thresholds are excluded from this slice. Use known secondary task facts as well as primary reasons so a blocked overdue task still matches an explicitly selected overdue condition.
- **Source truth/reliability:** reuse the complete bounded manager-scoped founder collector within the same transaction; UI pagination, overflow, errors and unavailable evidence cannot be treated as complete or as proof of resolution. Track stable source identity per rule/episode; changing titles, amounts or source versions does not create another issue. Only a positively observed clear source ends an episode, and a later observed recurrence opens a new one. Recheck current rule version/scope, granting authority, active recipient, source predicate, workspace/bot/link configuration immediately before a committed send claim. Explicitly route founder occurrences through the existing third-owner reminder dispatcher; commit SENDING before HTTP and release locks during network I/O. Preserve terminal FAILED/UNCERTAIN semantics and original receipts.
- **Legacy/manual-risk continuity:** explicit routing adoption stops legacy founder ALERT notifications, including already queued ones, so paused new rules do not silently reactivate old pings. Keep existing task alert records and all non-founder reminders. Include factual existing manually raised client/contract/budget concerns in the founder projection/catalog rather than inferring sentiment or performance; otherwise takeover would hide previously supported important risks. Telegram messages contain only chosen priority and a generic authenticated OS link, never client names, amounts, briefs, inbox text or provider payloads.
- **UI/UX:** add a focused Founder alert rules panel reached from Company overview, preserving queue filters and guarded drafts; no new sidebar. Separate read-only preview, save-paused and enable/adopt actions. Show condition, scope, recipient, priority and repeat summary before enabling. Distinguish configured/paused, current recipient eligibility, Telegram linking, bot/workspace settings, worker observation and actual occurrence status. Sent means Telegram API acceptance, not read. Display actionable missing setup and immutable history, with accessible desktop/390px and error/identity/conflict recovery.
- **Validation/limits:** verify concurrent observers/claims, source edits/clear/reopen/ack, secondary reasons, all matching pages, incomplete evidence, recipient/rule changes, legacy takeover, Redis recovery, slow network, terminal uncertainty and unchanged business sources. Generate additive reversible schema with history-preserving downgrade. Root owns one bounded heavy-check queue; source work runs in parallel. Use mocked sends and disposable fixtures only; no paid calls, actual messages or production activation. Live acceptance still requires the real bot/recipient connection. IMP-22 remains In progress until its full scope is accepted.

### 2026-10-28 — IMP-22 explicit alert follow-up accountability

- **Whole-item review:** some authoritative sources, especially client promises, have no stored responsible manager; a Telegram recipient alone does not establish accountability. Inventing an owner from project membership or historical handoff data would misrepresent the workflow. Adding general source-assignment fields across commitments, campaigns and finance would expand several separate domain changes and still leave imported/manual evidence gaps.
- **Chosen bounded responsibility model:** explicitly configure the rule's active administrator as both **alert follow-up owner and Telegram recipient**. The form and activation review must explain that this person reviews matching issues and arranges the next step. Show that current alert responsibility and eligibility on observed issues separately from the original source owner/unassigned state. ### 2026-10-29 — IMP-07 creative review decision

- **Business outcome/current → proposed workflow:** Amna and editors currently open an asset-level preview, type broad notes and switch to a distant task form for final approval. Final submission identities are already frozen on the server but omitted from frontend types. Put the exact submitted or explicitly selected production asset beside precise feedback: inspect/play → comment at a timestamp or on the asset → request changes through the existing task workflow → compare the next exact version → editor marks the thread addressed with evidence → current independent reviewer verifies/resolves → explicit final approval. Images/static ads, clips, audio and final videos need the same permitted review entry. Preserve every earlier asset/thread; new work never auto-resolves or silently retargets feedback.
- **Alternatives/chosen scope:** reuse native image/video/audio elements and existing authenticated no-store asset downloads, loading no more than two selected assets at once. Add cancellable fetches, byte/MIME bounds, loading/cancel/retry, unsupported-codec download and object-URL cleanup. Avoid arbitrary external embeds, a new public playback-token system, storage-driver range overhaul, synchronized editing or a canvas/NLE in this bounded review feature. These add risk/complexity without solving the immediate feedback handoff. Blob playback remains bounded by current 100 MB asset limits and needs download-before-play; state this limit. General image feedback and native time/time-range anchors support precise review without inventing verified duration/frame information.
- **Version/data contract:** two normalized tables hold version-bound feedback and append-only attributed events, separate from the creative JSON/production revision. Support exact SUBMISSION targets (frozen creativeVersion/finalAssetId) and ASSET targets (immutable asset UUID and brief/shot/production/assembly lineage) so intermediate media are covered too. Strict explicit selections never fallback to a current asset; legacy submissions without a frozen final identity remain unavailable. Commands use stable IDs, revision checks and exact target validation; feedback edits cannot change source production, money, AI budgets, fulfilment or approval.
- **Responsibility/approval policy:** permitted current task readers can add/reply, and the current assignee/manager can explicitly mark addressed with a reason and exact correction evidence. Only the current assigned manager-reviewer who is not the assignee resolves/reopens review feedback; departed authors do not strand the thread. Open notes are visible advisory evidence; do not invent a new automatic approval veto. Resolving a note never approves a deliverable. Keep the existing independent task review, required review checklist and revision-cost workflow; final decisions must name the exact latest submitted creative identity. Old submissions remain reviewable but cannot be approved as current.
- **UI/UX/navigation:** reuse the existing task drawer and Creative studio with a focused review region and explicit Media review entries, not another sidebar. The drawer is 640px: use labelled current/previous selectors and stacked players rather than unreadably small portrait columns; responsive layouts remain coherent at 390px. Put version, submitter, reviewer, source state and next action near the player. Use native accessible controls, no autoplay, keyboard-operable time links, clear General/At time labels, focused explicit editors and guarded drafts. Store exact submission/asset/comparison/feedback selection in URLs while preserving task/queue/panel filters. On current-access/API failure hide players and evidence, cancel pending loads and release URLs; retain only hidden same-identity drafts. Ignore late responses from old scopes; require explicit discard/reload for stale targets or navigation.
- **Acceptance before completion:** full v1 feedback → changes → v2 comparison → addressed evidence → independent resolution → explicit approval; intermediate image/general and audio/time feedback; original version/history preservation; no self-approval or implicit source writes; exact/current/legacy/missing/unsupported assets; cross-task/client/role revocation; stale source/feedback/command replay; concurrent comments/resolution; complete counts/paging; media cancellation and late-response cleanup; actual desktop/390px URL/back/keyboard/outage/draft journeys and exact synthetic-file/DB cleanup. Optional explicitly chosen wait hours extend the existing preview/save-paused/enable founder escalation rule; unknown/future timestamps cannot establish elapsed-age matching/clearing. ### 2026-10-29 — IMP-37/40 bounded primary task reads decision

- **Problem/business outcome:** ordinary work pages and the sidebar currently fetch all permitted task details, including historical submissions, media documents and activity. Final task mutations return the same full snapshot. Filtering a paginated-looking card list alone cannot reduce this hidden work. Staff need to find their next assignment quickly as client production/history grows; the founder needs complete counts rather than counts of whichever page happened to load. This is an independent engineering prerequisite for larger agent/media activity and does not require provider setup.
- **Current → proposed workflow:** use a verified task-free typed workspace context for identity/team/projects, a separate bounded server-filtered lightweight list with full-scope counts and explicit viewer calendar/timezone, and an exact on-demand task-detail query independent of current page membership. Sidebar navigation uses the already verified access source. Existing task writes run the same business command within its existing transaction and return a thin result, then explicitly refresh current details/lists. No global full-snapshot cache write or first-row fallback. Native tasks, approval, fulfilment, history, scope and write locks remain authoritative.
- **Alternatives/chosen boundary:** virtualizing cards while retaining the full snapshot would be cosmetic. Replacing all Operations JSON, every specialized picker and every history API in one change would broaden migration and regression risk. First migrate Today and My tasks with task creation/edit/submission/review/comment and exact drawer navigation; migrate Planner only with a separate complete current-plan capacity aggregate rather than deriving load from one task page. Specialized Clients, Creative cross-task references, Operations title lookup, Agents/history, Team/handoff and batch consumers keep explicitly isolated legacy reads until individually migrated. They must not be silently fed summary rows or invented empty details. A first accepted slice will make IMP-37 Partial; whole-item completion requires remaining consumers and history bounds. IMP-34 independent writes and IMP-40 broader JSON contracts remain Partial.
- **UI/UX/design:** reuse familiar navigation, task drawer, work-priority groups and approved-versus-client-delivery language. Distinguish complete filtered totals from visible-page counts. Provide labelled page navigation, reset cursors on filter/scope changes, preserve Back/Forward/selected task and protect drafts. Off-page source links load the requested task directly. Current identity/access/source failures hide old evidence and actions while same-identity drafts remain recoverable. Preserve accessible keyboard/mobile/empty/loading/retry flows; do not add another sidebar, invent turnaround targets or guess working hours. Keep legacy boundaries explicit in code and tracker, without implementation jargon in staff flows.
- **Cross-system/implementation tradeoffs:** a transitional exact-detail wrapper can carry one permitted legacy details document; this deliberately does not complete full history pagination or typed subdomains. Typed summary rows contain current state/owner/dates/priority/checklist/lifecycle only, with no brief/prompt/evidence/assets/history/costs. Counts and list rows share current permission/filter conditions. Cursors bind identity/scope/filter and deterministic order; current grants/project membership are rechecked. Do not change production/financial policy or introduce a second task model. Existing internal command callers remain compatible.
- **Acceptance before completion of the slice:** realistic isolated volumes with tied dates, multiple pages, large historical sentinel payloads and cross-workspace/project boundaries; full filter/group/state counts; stable paging; off-page/missing/foreign task detail; finance redaction/current role and project revocation; read-only source/history preservation and unchanged business writes. Prove SQL/results exclude full history for bootstrap/list/counts and cold migrated page plus task mutation creates no legacy snapshot query/response, including the sidebar. Measure actual payload differences without inventing an SLA. Relevant tests/types/lint/codegen and actual desktop/390px/back/draft/outage journeys are required, followed by exact fixture cleanup. No paid call, real message, ad/accounting mutation or deployment is needed.
- **Status:** IMP-37 is In progress;21 Local complete,13 Partial,1 In progress,11 Queued;25 unfinished. Continue without approval pauses.

### 2026-10-29 — IMP-12 retrospective assignment receipt race

Peer review of the new on-demand drawer exposed an existing production-receipt gap: a delayed seen request contains only taskId. If work moves from A to B and back to A before that request is handled, the old view can mark A's new assignment seen and suppress its reminder. A local callback epoch cannot protect server state. Reopen IMP-12 before relying on it in IMP-37.

Preserve the existing receipt/notice model and legacy API compatibility; add an optional exact expectedAssignmentKey built from the verified assignee ID and original stored assignmentAt (literal legacy marker only when absent). Compare it against current assignment under the existing lock before any seen-state/notice write. Both official drawer paths must send the key. A stale receipt should fail clearly/retry only after current verified source is available; it never completes work or marks individual messages read. Keep receipts independent from task-save outcomes and normal30second source refresh so typing/focus does not churn. Verify the actual A→B→A race leaves the new assignment unread, exact-current receipt works, legacy omission remains compatible and original once-only reminder cases pass. This is a bounded correctness repair with no new UI surface, transport or permission grant. Interim totals20 Local complete,13 Partial,2 In progress(12/37),11 Queued;26 unfinished whole items.

### 2026-10-29 — IMP-37 Planner capacity decision

- **Outcome/workflow:** managers need complete overload/unknown-planning evidence without downloading historical production or mistaking one task page for the whole team. Reuse the accepted task context/list/exact drawer and current plan/availability writes. Planner gets a complete manager-only seven-day workload aggregate, bounded teammate pages and a separate bounded assignment drilldown for a day or Needs planning. Availability, estimates, current review responsibility and task changes refresh the affected reads; no new working-hours assumption or productivity score is introduced.
- **Alternatives/design:** deriving capacity from25 visible task cards would be wrong. Fetching every full task for the grid would retain the hidden payload. Project only current plan/state/dates/owner/latest submission and exact current review assignment, and aggregate in PostgreSQL. Return at most25 teammates×seven days and at most25 assignment rows per drilldown, each with complete totals and clear paging. Keep capacity explicitly across all permitted projects, independent of card filters; use assignments where one task can contribute production and planned review. Board columns must label visible versus complete state counts rather than imply completeness. Reuse the current grid/table, unknown/overload labels and accessible task links; preserve drawer/modal draft recovery and mobile flows.
- **Semantics/permissions:** Approved reserves no remaining work. Pending review reserves only its effective review owner; other open work reserves assignee production plus canonical planned review. Working time sums production/revision/review while generation waits remain separate. Missing estimates/availability stay unknown; explicit zero stays known zero. Undated/before-start reservations appear under Needs planning; after-range work is outside the seven-day window. Preserve an explicit unavailable backup's reservation and show its eligibility warning, without falling back to primary; missing/inactive owners get an unresolved-owner count. Validate real calendar dates and seven-day overflow, scope/role/project/active-team changes and cursor identity on every read. Preserve existing independent review, per-slot availability revision conflicts and normalized plan commands; no new schema or write-policy change is intended.
- **Acceptance:** compare SQL aggregates/drilldowns with existing capacity semantics over multiple pages, tied order, heavy historical sentinels, missing/zero/malformed estimates, dual assignments, missing dates, exact latest backup/revocation, foreign scope and availability conflicts. Keep the throwing legacy-snapshot spy and prove Planner cold read/open/write/refetch has no full workspace operation. Inspect desktop390px/range/filter/paging/back/draft/outage flows and clean only exact owned fixtures. Remaining specialized consumers/history pagination stay open even after Planner is accepted. This decision authorizes implementation without another approval pause.

Planner refinement: a complete unresolved-owner warning needs actionable sources, not only a number. Add one bounded UNAVAILABLE_OWNER assignment bucket with memberId/date both null; DAY and NEEDS_PLANNING retain their required active-member/range combinations. The global warning and drilldown use the same scope of all current open unresolved-owner reservations, including future dates outside the seven-day grid, labelled explicitly. Bind the bucket/filter scope into its cursor. This adds no write/policy/schema and never transfers work to the primary reviewer automatically.

Planner data-quality refinement: the old availability table has no database bounds constraint, although its save API enforces0–1440minutes. A malformed stored value must become unknown on read, retaining its exact revision; it must not create false spare capacity. INVALID_AVAILABILITY is explicitly scoped to SELECTED_WEEK, while work/source warnings are OPEN_PLANNING. Return a source-derived availabilityInvalid flag on the exact day so the existing availability editor can repair that slot deliberately. Valid null, zero and absent slots remain distinguishable, and no source row is rewritten by reading it. The new summary/assignment API compiled and28 focused capacity/task cases passed before this final slot-label contract refinement; actual service/UI acceptance remains in progress.

### 2026-10-29 — IMP-37 Planner hierarchy correction during actual acceptance

The first actual 1440px Planner inspection exposed a material design gap: placing 25 complete-capacity teammate disclosures before the task list pushed daily assignments several screens down. This fails the manager's primary planning journey despite correct totals and bounded data. Use explicit URL-backed Tasks / Team workload views within Planner: Tasks opens assignments and its filters/board; Team workload opens the complete all-project capacity view and bounded drilldowns. Preserve task filters, exact selected work, Back/Forward and unsaved-draft confirmation across view changes. Existing capacity selection links must still open their workload context. A collapsed capacity card would shorten the page but keep two competing workflows and ambiguous task-filter scope; separate views give each a clear purpose without another sidebar item or service. This is a bounded IMP-37/06/11 hierarchy refinement, not whole-item completion for06/11. Check both desktop and390px journeys before accepting. The actual rollback-only capacity suite passed61 teammates,156 local/11 foreign tasks and78 complete day assignments, with no legacy snapshot/full-history hydration, no writes during reads, unchanged protected digests and no external calls. The initial ambiguous joined submission-version query was fixed before that passing run.

### 2026-10-29 — IMP-37 Planner refresh/concurrency review

Independent review found two gaps before acceptance. A full read-authorization/directory signature must mask stale capacity, but must not own an availability draft: an unrelated teammate rename or project refresh otherwise silently discards a manager's edits. Bind draft ownership to the verified workspace/member/account identity and guarded URL context; hide/disable unverified same-identity drafts and restore their exact input after verification. Real identity changes dispose private drafts. Separately, warning counts and exact day slots originally used separate READ COMMITTED statements, while availability correctly permits independent per-slot writes. Combine warnings and bounded member/day results in one PostgreSQL statement/MVCC snapshot rather than broadening the workspace lock and blocking unrelated planning. These choices protect the full draft/recovery workflow and coherent workload decisions without new permissions, APIs, history copies or global write contention. Source repairs are ready; final actual interleaving and desktop390px acceptance remain required.

### 2026-10-30 — IMP-37 Assistants read-boundary implementation decision

**Company/workflow:** staff need to find a permitted task assistant and recover its original findings; founder/Amna need complete scoped run counts/history and configuration review. The current five-second poll loads the entire delivery workspace, every configuration and the newest500 workspace runs with private results, then filters them. An employee's own older authorized run disappears behind other people's newer work. Raising that cap or hiding the transferred findings in collapsed cards would preserve the underlying defect.

**Chosen boundary:** a task-free typed assistant context/catalog, bounded search/filter pages and exact configuration; correctly scoped keyset run summaries with complete filtered counts, plus an exact run opened deliberately. Apply current project/native-task/initiator visibility before counts and pagination. Historical visibility must not depend on whether the current agent remains enabled or runnable. Share current actor/project access predicates with the existing engine rather than inventing another role model. Preserve existing execution, budget/knowledge/revision/independent-review, cancellation and queue-after-commit behavior behind thin typed commands; this change does not authorize an actual run, provider call or spending.

**UI/design:** separate catalog and history with URL selections/filters and explicit page recovery,25-row pages, narrow/keyboard use and exact details. Reuse genuine agent/task forms with their actual narrow inputs; never fabricate an empty DeliveryWorkspace just to satisfy props. A task picker must expose search/paging/total and retain exact selected-agent context; the first catalog page is not the whole directory. Current project configuration choices remain complete up to an explicit overflow guard. Load private instructions/context/findings only for an opened authorized detail. Poll lightweight sources normally and only current active/selected run summaries/details faster; preserve drafts and hide unverified private data during outages/revocation. Spending remains an explicit legacy boundary until its separate slice.

**Tradeoffs/acceptance:** keep legacy callers compatible while moving the normal Assistants entry and task assistant journey. Use existing matching indexes or add only justified generated additive indexes under the current version; do not rewrite previous migrations. Read count/page/detail under a consistent read transaction and bind cursors to identity/current scope/filters so invalidation offers First page rather than silently dropping work. Check an old own run behind>500 newer foreign runs, tied timestamps/multiple pages, native deletion/scope/initiator/role changes and disabled-agent history; prove no task/config/result-history projections in summary reads and no legacy snapshot operation on the new entry. Actual desktop320/390px, draft/Keep/outage/recovery journeys and protected exact fixture cleanup remain required. A new-agent configuration save has no existing create receipt, so an uncertain save must freeze and resolve safely rather than automatically allocate another identity. Whole-item counts are unchanged.

**Creation retry correction from independent review:** Apollo automatically retries a failed mutation once; a UI-only uncertain-save banner cannot prevent two newly allocated assistant IDs. The new typed create/duplicate command therefore supplies a stable explicit createId. Under current-admin authorization and the existing transaction lock, an exact schema-normalized version1 configuration may return the same current record without another write/audit; a different body or later version requires explicit current-record review. This is a bounded record-found replay, not an immutable original-creator receipt. New supplied IDs must use strict INSERT, never TypeORM save/upsert: a global primary-key collision outside the scoped lookup must not update or transfer another workspace's row. Existing update/version/provider/project/role checks and legacy callers remain compatible. Check same-ID concurrent/exact/different/revoked/edited/provider-unavailable and foreign-PK cases; no new paid execution is authorized.

**IMP-10 authority-loss correction identified during integration:** the shared draft guard intentionally continues pending navigation when the last dirty form becomes clean. A confirmed permission loss can instead unmount that form, which must never execute an old duplicate/edit callback carrying private configuration. Use a cancellation-only guard API that invalidates pending local callbacks and blocked browser navigation synchronously before their automatic continuation; it must not authorize a destination, skip confirmation or discard unrelated drafts. This works for embedded task assistance as well as the top-level page, unlike a dispatcher-only remount. Invoke it only on confirmed authority loss and clear the page's private create/run intents; temporary network outages retain hidden drafts and their normal recovery. Add pending-dialog and browser-back regressions plus existing guard/route tests. An original run body is owned only by its mounted preparation: reload/Back/regrant without that body remains Check/Close after an inconclusive request lookup, never editable defaults under an old request UUID. Engine request-body idempotency remains a separate23 follow-up; this slice must not silently reinterpret an old start intent.
